+    }

+  // Decode the request into host byte order.
+  if (reply.decode () == -1)
+    {
+      ACELIB_ERROR ((LM_ERROR,
+                     ACE_TEXT ("%p\n"),
+                     ACE_TEXT ("decode failed")));
